673:def smoke_block(block, out, bridge, t2_draft, allow_agent, payloads):
674:    """--smoke: one request per block, own server up and down, no 180 s loop or gate wait beyond
675:    the single telemetry reading already needed to launch. Always labelled SMOKE — NOT VALID TIMING."""
676:    record = dict(block=block, label=SMOKE_LABEL, command=command(block, t2_draft),
677:                  started_epoch=time.time(), server_started=False, reply_received=False,
678:                  exit_clean=None, image_tokens=None, reason=None, allow_agent=allow_agent)
679:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
680:    logpath = out / f'{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
681:    proc = pid = None
682:    try:
683:        record['reading'] = telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=allow_agent)
684:        free_ports()
685:        if not allow_agent:
686:            matches = agent_matches()
687:            if matches:
688:                log_agent_matches(matches)
689:                raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
690:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
691:            began = time.monotonic()
692:            proc = spawn(record['command'], log)
693:            pid = None
694:            while pid is None:
695:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - began > 10:
696:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
697:                try:
698:                    pid = server_pid(proc)
699:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
700:                    time.sleep(.05)
701:            record['server_started'] = True
702:            write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
703:            while True:
704:                if proc.poll() is not None:
705:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
706:                try:
707:                    if post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
708:                        break
709:                except (OSError, ValueError):
710:                    pass
711:                if time.monotonic() - began >= 180:
712:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
713:                time.sleep(.2)
714:            check_cores(pid)
715:            endpoint = '/completion' if block.startswith('T') else '/v1/chat/completions'
716:            name, body = payloads[0]
717:            offset = logpath.stat().st_size
718:            t0 = time.monotonic()
719:            data = post(endpoint, body, timeout=180)  # same bound as the real warm-up; V4 (BF16) took 55-72 s
720:            end = time.monotonic()
721:            with logpath.open() as f:
722:                f.seek(offset)
723:                delta = f.read()
724:            row = request_record(name, data, t0, end, delta, block)
725:            record['reply_received'] = True
726:            record['image_tokens'] = row.get('image_tokens')
727:            record['requests'] = [row]
728:    except Exception as e:
729:        record['reason'] = str(e)
730:    finally:
731:        write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=None))
732:        if proc is not None:
733:            # One TERM to the server lets it exit 0; clean = exit 0 and nothing left in our group.
734:            record['server_exit'] = own_stop(proc, pid)
735:            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not group_alive(proc.pid)
736:        write(out / f'{block}_{attempt}.json', record)
737:    return record

/bin/bash -lc "rg -n '"'^'"' docs/CLAUDE.md" in /data/data/com.termux/files/home/review/vis3/round2_copy
 succeeded in 0ms:
1:# CLAUDE.md
2:
3:Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
4:
5:**Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
6:
7:## 1. Think Before Coding
8:
9:**Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
10:
11:Before implementing:
12:- State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
13:- If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
14:- If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
15:- If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
16:
17:## 2. Simplicity First
18:
19:**Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
20:
21:- No features beyond what was asked.
22:- No abstractions for single-use code.
23:- No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
24:- No error handling for impossible scenarios.
25:- If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
26:
27:Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
28:
29:## 3. Surgical Changes
30:
31:**Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
32:
33:When editing existing code:
34:- Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
35:- Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
36:- Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
37:- If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
38:
39:When your changes create orphans:
40:- Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
41:- Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
42:
43:The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
44:
45:## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
46:
47:**Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
48:
49:Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
50:- "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
51:- "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
52:- "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
53:
54:For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
55:```
56:1. [Step] → verify: [check]
57:2. [Step] → verify: [check]
58:3. [Step] → verify: [check]
59:```
60:
61:Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
62:
63:## 5. Context Discipline
64:
65:**Context is a budget. Spend it on reasoning, not on re-reading.**
66:
67:- Never re-read a full file already open in this session. Reference what
68:  you already have. If you need one function, quote that function - not
69:  the file.
70:- Summarize benchmark results as a single row, not raw logs.
71:  Good: `E2B Q4_K_M | 4 threads | 11-12 tok/s | thermal=PENDING`
72:  Bad: pasting 15 lines of per-cycle output.
73:- Don't paste full stack traces. Extract only the failing line and the
74:  exception type. The other 20 frames are noise.
75:  Good: `stereo_depth.py:627 ZeroDivisionError - height_px was 0`
76:  Bad: the entire traceback.
77:- Terminal output: paste the result, not the scrollback. Server boot logs,
78:  model metadata dumps, and `cmake` output are almost never the signal.
79:- When a command produces >20 lines, pipe it: `| tail -5`, `| grep ERROR`,
80:  or write a summary block at the end of the script.
81:
82:---
83:
84:## Project-Specific Constraints
85:
86:These values are **benchmarked on this hardware**. Do not change them without
87:re-running the corresponding benchmark and recording the result in DECISIONS.md.
88:
89:**llama.cpp server flags** (`server_manager.py`):
90:```
91:--threads 4 --threads-batch 4    # optimal for Tensor G2; more threads = slower
92:--parallel 1                     # no slot splitting
93:--swa-full                       # fixes Gemma 4 SWA prompt-cache invalidation
94:--ctx-size 2048
95:```
96:
97:**Accuracy target and historical context:**
98:```
99:Voice command parsing:  ≥93%
100:```
101:
102:The earlier ≥90% LLM-navigation target described the model-in-loop design.
103:DECISIONS #104 decided to remove the LLM from the low-level navigation loop;
104:the code rework is still pending (STATUS Architecture). Do not treat that
105:historical target as a current model-selection gate.
106:
107:**Vision** (`detect_person.py`):
108:```
109:MODEL = yolo11s_320.onnx + yolo11s_640.onnx (stock COCO; yolo11m.onnx kept for rollback)
110:CONF  = 0.35
111:IOU   = 0.45
112:Input = 320 or 640 per frame (SizePolicy); camera = RobotCam 640x480, 1/s; robot detector 2 threads pinned to MID cores 4–5 (#136)
113:Detector dicts: source-frame pixels; legacy tuples: 640 space
114:```
115:See DECISIONS #121, #122.
116:
117:Anything marked `UNCALIBRATED` or `PENDING` in STATUS.md is not yet trustworthy —
118:treat those values as placeholders, not as benchmarked constants.
119:
120:
121:## Workflow and commit gate
122:
123:Before code work read `AGENTS.md`, `docs/WORKFLOW.md`, and the assigned role file.
124:As Coder, obtain the independent review automatically, preserve it verbatim, and
125:wait for the human's explicit commit decision. A reviewer pass is not commit
126:permission; push needs separate authorization. This does not alter this file's
127:prototype-Python scope.

/bin/bash -lc "rg -n '"'^'"' review_inputs/round1_last_message.txt" in /data/data/com.termux/files/home/review/vis3/round2_copy
 succeeded in 0ms:
1:Found five P2 issues that should be addressed before timed owner runs. No P0 or P1 finding. Review remained read-only; no servers, benchmarks, motors, or other reviewers were launched.
2:
3:Reviewed candidate and shared harness line counts:
4:
5:| File | Lines |
6:|---|---:|
7:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py` | 530 |
8:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/test_vis3.py` | 288 |
9:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh` | 110 |
10:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md` | 75 |
11:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py` | 489 |
12:| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py` | 838 |
13:| `review_inputs/DIAGNOSIS.md` | 16 |
14:
15:Below, `vis3_run.py` means `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py`.
16:
17:1. **P2 — Effective runtime settings can differ while pooled runs pass comparability.**
18:
19:   `vis3_run.py:337`:
20:   ```python
21:        if os.environ.get('LD_LIBRARY_PATH'):
22:   ```
23:
24:   `vis3_run.py:403`:
25:   ```python
26:    keys = ('code_sha256', 'payload_sha256', 'weight_sha256', 'binary_sha256', 'rounds', 'first_n', 'skin_start_max')
27:   ```
28:
29:   Preflight checks library search paths, but neither rejects nor records llama/OpenMP environment overrides. Batch size, ubatch size, flash attention, and loading mode remain implicit. Both upstream argument parsers accept environment overrides for these settings.
30:
31:   Concrete scenario: separately launched O and N folders use different container environment settings for `LLAMA_ARG_UBATCH`. Their manifests still compare equal, and identical token counts do not reveal the different prompt-processing configuration. A legacy mmap environment override can also affect OLD while NEW has removed that option.
32:
33:   Reject relevant overrides or explicitly control and record effective settings; include them in pooling checks. Add a regression covering different effective configurations.
34:
35:2. **P2 — Warm-phase throughput can be thermally confounded without the summary exposing its starting conditions.**
36:
37:   `vis3_run.py:237`:
38:   ```python
39:        for name in ('cold', 'warm'):  # warm = immediate relaunch, files still in the page cache
40:   ```
41:
42:   `vis3_run.py:383`:
43:   ```python
44:        gate_skin=(d.get('gate') or {}).get('skin'), phases=phase,
45:   ```
46:
47:   The strengthened gate runs before the cold phase. Cold loading, initialization, and the first completion then heat the phone before the immediate warm launch. The summary’s start-skin comparison uses the earlier gate reading, rather than either phase’s actual launch temperature.
48:
49:   Concrete scenario: OLD and NEW both pass at 31 °C, but their cold initialization leaves different temperatures or frequency caps. Warm throughput then differs partly because of that carry-over, while reported start-skin spread is zero.
50:
51:   Gate the warm launch without dropping caches, and assess actual per-phase starting temperatures/caps. The existing CRITICAL and battery stops remain intact; this finding concerns comparison validity.
52:
53:3. **P2 — A failed mapped-library check does not reject the block.**
54:
55:   `vis3_run.py:248`:
56:   ```python
57:            phase['libs'] = mapped_libs(pid, build)
58:   ```
59:
60:   `vis3_run.py:274`:
61:   ```python
62:        record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed cold + warm sequence')
63:   ```
64:
65:   A false `libs['ok']` is only reported later by summary checks. Execution continues through the measurement sequence and can receive `VALID`; aggregate configuration medians and NEW/OLD ratios still include it.
66:
67:   Concrete scenario: a launcher maps a ggml/llama library from another build directory. The harness detects the mismatch but continues measuring a configuration outside the intended OLD/NEW comparison.
68:
69:   Raise immediately on a failed library check, preserving the evidence in the failed record. Add a lifecycle test with `mapped_libs(...).ok == False`.
70:
71:4. **P2 — The added intermediate shutdown races with the native memory sampler.**
72:
73:   `vis3_run.py:198`:
74:   ```python
75:    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
76:   ```
77:
78:   `vis3_run.py:289`:
79:   ```python
80:            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
81:   ```
82:
83:   Clearing `target.json` does not acknowledge that an already-running monitor sample has finished. That sample may still hold the cold server PID when shutdown removes its `/proc` files. The reused monitor then records a sampling failure.
84:
85:   Unlike VIS-2’s final shutdown, this cold-phase shutdown occurs inside the interval checked for sampling errors. A normal, clean exit can therefore invalidate the completed block, or transient error telemetry can abort the warm launch.
86:
87:   Synchronize sampler detachment before stopping the server. Keep sampling failures during live measurement fatal. Add a test covering an in-flight sample during cold-to-warm shutdown.
88:
89:5. **P2 — Preflight can declare readiness with a required Python dependency missing.**
90:
91:   `vis3_run.py:351`:
92:   ```python
93:    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
94:   ```
95:
96:   `chat.py:1`:
97:   ```python
98:import sys, os, requests, time, subprocess, shutil
99:   ```
100:
101:   VIS-3 removed the Debian dependency check inherited by VIS-2, but payload construction still reaches `chat.py` through `v1.text_payload()`.
102:
103:   Concrete scenario: Debian lacks `requests`. Both wrapper preflights pass and readiness is announced; the owner subsequently waits through the five-minute idle before payload construction fails.
104:
105:   Check the dependencies actually needed by this text-only harness before reporting readiness. Pillow need not remain required. Add a missing-`requests` preflight test.
106:
107:The diagnosis is broadly supported for the listed compiler/CPU build settings, draft-thread inheritance, disabled per-request speculative adjustments, buffered projector reads, combined teardown, and disabled subprocess support. However, “identical logic” overstates the speculative conversion comparison: NEW contains additional behavior, although its thread-inheritance branch matches OLD. The archived help also distinguishes OLD’s `mmap` default from NEW’s `auto`; equivalent behavior on this CPU should be described separately from identical defaults.

7:+"""VIS-3: b1609-dotprod vs b11716 runtime parity on the same GGUF, no projector; owner-only, motors off.
8:+
9:+Reuses VIS-2 (vis2_run.py, imported unchanged) and through it VIS-1. --dry-run and --smoke are NOT VALID TIMING.
10:+"""
11:+import argparse
12:+from datetime import datetime, timezone
13:+import hashlib
14:+import importlib.util
15:+import json
16:+import os
17:+from pathlib import Path
18:+import re
19:+import signal
20:+import statistics
21:+import subprocess
22:+import sys
23:+import time
24:+
25:+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
26:+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
27:+REPO = HERE.parents[2]
28:+_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('vis2_run', REPO/'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py')
29:+v2 = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
30:+_spec.loader.exec_module(v2)
31:+v1 = v2.v1
32:+
33:+# block -> (build key in v1.BINS, MTP drafter on). O/N is the essential pair; OM/NM is optional.
34:+BLOCKS = dict(O=('b1609-dotprod', False), N=('vis', False), OM=('b1609-dotprod', True), NM=('vis', True))
35:+NAMES = {'b1609-dotprod': 'OLD b1609', 'vis': 'NEW b11716'}
36:+WEIGHTS = {p: v1.EXPECTED_HASHES[p] for p in (v1.MODEL, v1.DRAFT)}
37:+ROUNDS = 4            # 4 x (uncached, cached, generation) per block; O,N,N,O gives 8 per metric per build
38:+FIRST_N = 16          # first-response request: gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens
39:+BLOCK_CAP_S = 600
40:+REQ_TIMEOUT_S = 120
41:+LOAD_TIMEOUT_S = 180
42:+# Calibration knob: absolute start ceiling on top of VIS-1's idle + 1.5 C. VIS-1/2 idles ranged 27-36.7 C and
43:+# b1609's T1 started at 39 C with the X1 capped; #126 saw the first CPU cap near 32 C skin.
44:+SKIN_START_MAX = 32.0
45:+GATE_WAIT_S = 1800
46:+POLICIES = ('policy4', 'policy6')  # cores 4-5 (A78) and 6-7 (X1) = taskset 4-7
47:+CPUFREQ = Path('/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq')
48:+SMOKE_LABEL = v2.SMOKE_LABEL
49:+DRY_LABEL = v2.DRY_LABEL
50:+# First occurrence of each line in the default-verbosity server log; both builds print all of them.
51:+MARKERS = dict(load_start='load_model: loading model', vocab="control-looking token", kv_cache='llama_kv_cache_iswa:',
52:+               draft_start='common_speculative_init_result: loading draft model', loaded='llama_server: model loaded')
53:+STAMP = re.compile(r'^(\d+)\.(\d\d)\.(\d{3})\.(\d{3}) ')
54:+LIB = re.compile(r'(/\S*/lib(?:ggml|llama|mtmd)[^/\s]*\.so[.\d]*)$')
55:+
56:+
57:+def command(block):
58:+    """server_manager.start_server flags for setup_q4 (ctx 2048), plus the VIS harness's taskset/timeout/port."""
59:+    build, mtp = BLOCKS[block]
60:+    cmd = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900', str(v1.BINS[build]),
61:+           '-m', str(v1.MODEL), '--port', '8091', '--ctx-size', '2048', '--threads', '4', '--threads-batch', '4',
62:+           '--parallel', '1', '--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '0', '--host', '127.0.0.1']
63:+    return cmd + (v1.DRAFT_ARGS if mtp else [])
64:+
65:+
66:+def blocks(arg):
67:+    sel = arg.split(',')
68:+    if not sel or any(b not in BLOCKS for b in sel):
69:+        raise ValueError('--blocks takes a comma list of O,N,OM,NM (repeats allowed, e.g. O,N,N,O)')
70:+    return sel
71:+
72:+
73:+def payloads():
74:+    gen, unc, cached = v2.payloads()
75:+    first = ('F_' + gen[0][0], dict(gen[0][1], n_predict=FIRST_N))
76:+    return gen[:ROUNDS], unc, cached[:ROUNDS], first
77:+
78:+
79:+def sequence(smoke, gen, unc, cached):
80:+    """(kind, name, endpoint, body); identical for every block."""
81:+    seq = []
82:+    for k in range(1 if smoke else ROUNDS):
83:+        seq += [('uncached', *unc), ('cached', *cached[k]), ('gen', *gen[k])]
84:+    return [(kind, name, '/completion', body) for kind, name, body in seq]
85:+
86:+
87:+def stamp_s(m):
88:+    mi, se, ms, us = map(int, m.groups())
89:+    return round(mi*60 + se + ms/1e3 + us/1e6, 3)
90:+
91:+
92:+def log_markers(text):
93:+    out = {}
94:+    for line in text.splitlines():
95:+        m = STAMP.match(line)
96:+        if m:
97:+            for key, needle in MARKERS.items():
98:+                if key not in out and needle in line:
99:+                    out[key] = stamp_s(m)
100:+    return out
101:+
102:+
103:+def freq_state(root=CPUFREQ):
104:+    """cpufreq time_in_state (10 ms ticks per frequency); readable without root."""
105:+    state = {}
106:+    for p in POLICIES:
107:+        rows = (line.split() for line in (root/p/'stats/time_in_state').read_text().splitlines() if line.strip())
108:+        state[p] = {int(f): int(t) for f, t in rows}
109:+    return state
110:+
111:+
112:+def freq_delta(a, b):
113:+    """Mean policy frequency (MHz) and share of time at its top frequency between two snapshots."""
114:+    out = {}
115:+    for p in POLICIES:
116:+        d = {f: b[p][f] - a[p].get(f, 0) for f in b[p]}
117:+        total = sum(d.values())
118:+        out[p] = dict(ticks=total, mean_mhz=round(sum(f*t for f, t in d.items())/total/1000) if total > 0 else None,
119:+                      top_share=round(d[max(d)]/total, 3) if total > 0 else None)
120:+    return out
121:+
122:+
123:+def mapped_libs(pid, build, maps=None):
124:+    """llama/ggml/mtmd libraries the running server actually mapped; all must come from its own build."""
125:+    text = maps if maps is not None else Path(f'/proc/{pid}/maps').read_text()
126:+    libs = sorted({m.group(1) for line in text.splitlines() if (m := LIB.search(line))})
127:+    want = str(v1.BINS[build].parent) + '/'
128:+    return dict(libs=libs, ok=bool(libs) and all(x.startswith(want) for x in libs))
129:+
130:+
131:+def verify(block):
132:+    """The running server must report no projector, the intended MTP state and ctx 2048; never adjust flags."""
133:+    mtp = BLOCKS[block][1]
134:+    props, slots = v2.get('/props'), v2.get('/slots')
135:+    got = dict(vision=props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision'), speculative=[s.get('speculative') for s in slots],
136:+               n_ctx=[s.get('n_ctx') for s in slots], build_info=props.get('build_info'))
137:+    if got['vision'] is not False or got['speculative'] != [mtp] or got['n_ctx'] != [2048]:
138:+        raise RuntimeError(f'configuration mismatch: expected vision=False mtp={mtp} n_ctx=2048, server reports {got}')
139:+    return got
140:+
141:+
142:+def gate_ok(d, idle):
143:+    return v1.gate_ok(d['skin'], idle) and d['skin'] <= SKIN_START_MAX
144:+
145:+
146:+def gate(bridge, idle, now=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
147:+    """VIS-2 gate (idle + 1.5 C, all telemetry stops) plus the absolute SKIN_START_MAX ceiling."""
148:+    start = now()
149:+    while True:
150:+        d = v2.telemetry(bridge)
151:+        if gate_ok(d, idle):
152:+            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=now()-start, max=d.get('max'),
153:+                        cpuinfo_max_khz=d.get('cpuinfo_max_khz'))
154:+        if now()-start >= GATE_WAIT_S:
155:+            raise RuntimeError(f'gate missed: skin not <= min(idle+1.5, {SKIN_START_MAX}) C after {GATE_WAIT_S} s')
156:+        sleep(5)
157:+
158:+
159:+def drop_caches(bridge):
160:+    """Same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1/VIS-2."""
161:+    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
162:+    (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
163:+    began = time.monotonic()
164:+    while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
165:+        if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
166:+            raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
167:+        time.sleep(.1)
168:+    if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
169:+        raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
170:+    (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
171:+    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
172:+
173:+
174:+def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
175:+    """Spawn the owned server and wait for /health; load_s = spawn to /health ok (server readiness)."""
176:+    v1.free_ports()
177:+    with logpath.open('w') as log:
178:+        began = time.monotonic()
179:+        live['proc'] = proc = v1.spawn(cmd, log)
180:+    while live.get('pid') is None:
181:+        if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
182:+            raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
183:+        try:
184:+            live['pid'] = v1.server_pid(proc)
185:+        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
186:+            time.sleep(.05)
187:+    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=live['pid']))
188:+    while True:
189:+        if proc.poll() is not None:
190:+            raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
191:+        try:
192:+            if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
193:+                break
194:+        except (OSError, ValueError):
195:+            pass
196:+        v2.telemetry(bridge)
197:+        if time.monotonic()-began >= LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
198:+            raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
199:+        time.sleep(.05)
200:+    return time.monotonic()-began
201:+
202:+
203:+def detach(bridge, wait=5):
204:+    """Clear the monitor target, then wait for a sample begun after that, so no in-flight sample still reads the
205:+    server's /proc files when it exits (the cold stop lies inside the block's sampling-error window)."""
206:+    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
207:+    after, deadline = time.monotonic(), time.monotonic() + wait
208:+    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
209:+        if any(s.get('t_start', -1) > after for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')[-3:]):
210:+            return True
211:+        time.sleep(.1)
212:+    return False
213:+
214:+
215:+def stop(live, bridge, phase):
216:+    proc, pid = live.pop('proc', None), live.pop('pid', None)
217:+    if proc is None:
218:+        v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
219:+    else:
220:+        phase['sampler_detached'] = detach(bridge)
221:+        began = time.monotonic()
222:+        phase['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
223:+        phase['stop_s'] = time.monotonic()-began
224:+        phase['exit_clean'] = phase['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
225:+
226:+
227:+def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
228:+    offset = logpath.stat().st_size
229:+    f0, t = freq_state(), time.monotonic()
230:+    data = v2.guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout)
231:+    end, f1 = time.monotonic(), freq_state()
232:+    with logpath.open() as f:
233:+        f.seek(offset)
234:+        delta = f.read()
235:+    row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'T2')
236:+    row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
237:+               prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'), freq=freq_delta(f0, f1))
238:+    return row
239:+
240:+
241:+def run_block(block, index, out, bridge, idle, seq, first, smoke):
242:+    build, mtp = BLOCKS[block]
243:+    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, index=index, build=build, mtp=mtp,
244:+                  label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[], phases={},
245:+                  command=command(block), started_epoch=time.time(), smoke=smoke)
246:+    stem = f'{index:02d}_{block}_' + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
247:+    path = out/f'{stem}.json'
248:+    live, phase = {}, {}
249:+    try:
250:+        if not smoke:
251:+            record['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
252:+        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
253:+            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
254:+        if not smoke:  # cold: the GGUF and libraries leave the page cache
255:+            drop_caches(bridge)
256:+            record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge, idle)
257:+        for name in ('cold', 'warm'):  # warm = relaunch without a cache drop, files still in the page cache
258:+            phase = record['phases'][name] = {}
259:+            if name == 'warm' and not smoke:  # cold load and first reply heat the phone: gate again
260:+                phase['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
261:+            d = v2.telemetry(bridge)
262:+            phase['start'] = dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], capacity=d['capacity'],
263:+                                  voltage_now_uV=d['voltage_now_uV'], mem_available_kib=d.get('mem_available_kib'),
264:+                                  max=d.get('max'))
265:+            logpath = out/f'{stem}.{name}.server.log'
266:+            phase['load_s'] = launch(record['command'], logpath, bridge, live)
267:+            pid = live['pid']
268:+            v1.check_cores(pid)
269:+            phase['reported'] = verify(block)
270:+            phase['libs'] = mapped_libs(pid, build)
271:+            if not phase['libs']['ok']:
272:+                raise RuntimeError(f'server mapped libraries outside its build: {phase["libs"]["libs"]}')
273:+            phase['pss_after_load'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
274:+            phase['first'] = timed(bridge, pid, logpath, 'first', first[0], '/completion', first[1], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)
275:+            if name == 'warm':
276:+                record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
277:+                for kind, req, endpoint, body in seq:
278:+                    v2.telemetry(bridge)
279:+                    v1.check_cores(pid)
280:+                    left = BLOCK_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
281:+                    if left <= 0:
282:+                        raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
283:+                    record['requests'].append(timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, req, endpoint, body,
284:+                                                    min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left)))
285:+                    if time.monotonic()-start > BLOCK_CAP_S:
286:+                        raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
287:+                    v1.write(path, record)
288:+                record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
289:+                phase['pss_end'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
290:+            if live['proc'].poll() is not None:
291:+                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during ' + name)
292:+            v1.check_cores(pid)
293:+            v2.telemetry(bridge)
294:+            stop(live, bridge, phase)
295:+            phase['markers'] = log_markers(logpath.read_text(errors='replace'))
296:+            if not phase['exit_clean']:
297:+                raise RuntimeError(f"{name} server cleanup not clean (exit {phase['server_exit']})")
298:+        record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed cold + warm sequence')
299:+    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
300:+        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
301:+    finally:
302:+        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
303:+        stop(live, bridge, phase)
304:+        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
305:+        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
306:+                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
307:+        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
308:+        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
309:+        if record['label'] != 'NOT VALID':
310:+            bad = [] if record['lmk'].get('ok') else ['LMK query failed']
311:+            if v2.sample_violations(record['samples']):
312:+                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(v2.sample_violations(record['samples'])))
313:+            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
314:+                bad.append('sampling failed')
315:+            if bad:
316:+                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
317:+        v1.write(path, record)
318:+    return record
319:+
320:+
321:+def code_paths():
322:+    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh'] + v2.code_paths()
323:+
324:+
325:+def libs_of(build):
326:+    return sorted(v1.BINS[build].parent.glob('lib*.so*'))
327:+
328:+
329:+def manifest(selected, smoke, gen, unc, cached, first):
330:+    allp = gen + [unc] + cached + [first]
331:+    return dict(blocks=selected, smoke=smoke, rounds=ROUNDS, first_n=FIRST_N, block_cap_s=BLOCK_CAP_S,
332:+                skin_start_max=SKIN_START_MAX, commands={b: command(b) for b in BLOCKS},
333:+                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n, p in allp},
334:+                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
335:+                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
336:+                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for b in BLOCKS.values() for p in [v1.BINS[b[0]]] + libs_of(b[0])},
337:+                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
338:+                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))
339:+
340:+
341:+ENV_OVERRIDE = re.compile(r'^(LLAMA_|GGML_|OMP_|KMP_|LD_LIBRARY_PATH$|LD_PRELOAD$)')
342:+
343:+
344:+def check_env(env=os.environ):
345:+    """Both servers read LLAMA_ARG_* (batch, ubatch, flash attention, mmap, ...) and OpenMP variables from the
346:+    environment, which the command line would not show; refuse any so both run on their defaults."""
347:+    found = sorted(k for k in env if ENV_OVERRIDE.match(k))
348:+    if found:
349:+        raise SystemExit('environment overrides server settings; unset: ' + ', '.join(found))
350:+
351:+
352:+def preflight():
353:+    """Before and after the 5-minute idle: ports, agents, files/hashes, no shadowing libs, plus Debian or native checks."""
354:+    v1.free_ports()
355:+    matches = v1.agent_matches()
356:+    if matches:
357:+        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
358:+        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
359:+    missing = [str(p) for p in (*WEIGHTS, *v1.BINS.values()) if not p.is_file()]
360:+    missing += [f'{b} shared libraries' for b in v1.BINS if not libs_of(b)]
361:+    if missing:
362:+        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing))
363:+    bad = [str(p) for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if v1.sha(p) != h]
364:+    if bad:
365:+        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(bad))
366:+    # Both launchers list $PREFIX/lib before their own build dir in RUNPATH: a packaged libllama would shadow both.
367:+    shadow = sorted(p.name for p in (v1.NATIVE.parent/'usr/lib').glob('lib*') if re.match(r'lib(ggml|llama|mtmd)', p.name))
368:+    if shadow:
369:+        raise SystemExit('Termux $PREFIX/lib would shadow the build libraries: ' + ', '.join(shadow))
370:+    freq_state()
371:+    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
372:+        check_env()
373:+        if importlib.util.find_spec('requests') is None:
374:+            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing requests (chat.py imports it) — run: apt-get install python3-requests')
375:+    else:
376:+        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
377:+            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
378:+        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
379:+        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
380:+            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
381:+        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
382:+        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
383:+        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
384:+        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
385:+        if not stuck:
386:+            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
387:+    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
388:+
389:+
390:+def med(xs):
391:+    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
392:+    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
393:+
394:+
395:+def mib(snap):
396:+    return snap['pss_kib'] and round(snap['pss_kib']/1024) if snap else None
397:+
398:+
399:+def gen_mhz(rows, policy):
400:+    """Tick-weighted mean frequency of one policy over the given requests."""
401:+    ticks = sum(r['freq'][policy]['ticks'] for r in rows if r.get('freq'))
402:+    khz = sum(r['freq'][policy]['ticks'] * (r['freq'][policy]['mean_mhz'] or 0) for r in rows if r.get('freq'))
403:+    return round(khz/ticks) if ticks else None
404:+
405:+
406:+def block_line(d):
407:+    r, m, ph = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {}), d.get('phases', {})
408:+    by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
409:+    dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
410:+    da = sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
411:+    phase = {k: dict(load_s=med([p.get('load_s')]), first_ms=p.get('first') and round(p['first']['wall_ms']),
412:+                     first_prompt_ms=p.get('first') and p['first'].get('prompt_ms'), markers=p.get('markers'),
413:+                     pss_after_load_mib=mib(p.get('pss_after_load')), exit_clean=p.get('exit_clean'),
414:+                     stop_s=p.get('stop_s') and round(p['stop_s'], 2), libs_ok=(p.get('libs') or {}).get('ok'),
415:+                     build_info=(p.get('reported') or {}).get('build_info'), start_skin=(p.get('start') or {}).get('skin'))
416:+             for k, p in ph.items()}
417:+    return dict(
418:+        block=f"{d['index']:02d}_{d['block']}", build=d['build'], mtp=d['mtp'], label=d['label'], reason=d['reason'],
419:+        gate_skin=(d.get('gate') or {}).get('skin'), phases=phase,
420:+        uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
421:+        uncached_prompt_n=sorted({x['prompt_tokens'] for x in by('uncached')}),
422:+        cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
423:+        gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
424:+        gen_tokens={x['input']: x['generated_tokens'] for x in by('gen')},
425:+        prompt_n={x['input']: x['prompt_tokens'] for x in r},
426:+        draft_acceptance=round(da/dn, 3) if dn else None,
427:+        gen_mean_mhz={p: gen_mhz(by('gen'), p) for p in POLICIES},
428:+        checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
429:+                    cached_reuse=bool(by('cached')) and all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
430:+                    replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r),
431:+                    libs_ok=bool(phase) and all(p['libs_ok'] for p in phase.values())),
432:+        peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
433:+        min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
434:+        lmk=d.get('lmk'), skin=[m.get('skin_start'), m.get('skin_end')], status_max=m.get('status_max'),
435:+        caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()}, power=m.get('power'))
436:+
437:+
438:+def spread(xs):
439:+    return round(max(xs) - min(xs), 2) if xs and None not in xs else None
440:+
441:+
442:+def comparable(manifests):
443:+    keys = ('code_sha256', 'payload_sha256', 'weight_sha256', 'binary_sha256', 'commands', 'rounds', 'first_n',
444:+            'skin_start_max')
445:+    return all({k: x[k] for k in keys} == {k: manifests[0][k] for k in keys} for x in manifests)
446:+
447:+
448:+def summarize(*folders):
449:+    """Per-block lines and per-config medians over VALID blocks from saved records only; folders may be pooled."""
450:+    manifests = [json.loads((Path(f)/'run.json').read_text())['manifest'] for f in folders]
451:+    if not comparable(manifests):
452:+        raise SystemExit('folders differ in code, payloads, weights, binaries or constants; not pooled')
453:+    records = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for f in folders for p in sorted(Path(f).glob('[0-9][0-9]_*Z.json'))]
454:+    lines = [block_line(d) for d in records]
455:+    for line in lines:
456:+        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
457:+    valid = [(d, x) for d, x in zip(records, lines) if x['label'] == 'VALID']
458:+    configs = {}
459:+    for mtp in (False, True):
460:+        for build in NAMES:
461:+            rs = [d for d, x in valid if x['build'] == build and x['mtp'] == mtp]
462:+            if not rs:
463:+                continue
464:+            req = lambda k: [q for d in rs for q in d['requests'] if q['kind'] == k]
465:+            ph = lambda name, key: [d['phases'][name].get(key) for d in rs]
466:+            dn = sum(q.get('draft_n') or 0 for q in req('gen'))
467:+            configs[(build, mtp)] = dict(
468:+                build=NAMES[build], mtp=mtp, blocks=len(rs), load_cold_s=med(ph('cold', 'load_s')),
469:+                load_warm_s=med(ph('warm', 'load_s')),
470:+                first_cold_ms=med([p['wall_ms'] for p in ph('cold', 'first')]),
471:+                first_warm_ms=med([p['wall_ms'] for p in ph('warm', 'first')]),
472:+                uncached_pp_tok_s=med([q['prompt_per_second'] for q in req('uncached')]),
473:+                cached_ms=med([q['prompt_ms'] for q in req('cached')]),
474:+                gen_tok_s=med([q['predicted_per_second'] for q in req('gen')]),
475:+                draft_acceptance=round(sum(q.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for q in req('gen'))/dn, 3) if dn else None,
476:+                gen_mean_mhz={p: gen_mhz(req('gen'), p) for p in POLICIES},
477:+                pss_after_load_mib=med([mib(p) for p in ph('warm', 'pss_after_load')]),
478:+                peak_pss_mib=med([x['peak_pss_mib'] for _, x in valid if x['build'] == build and x['mtp'] == mtp]),
479:+                min_mem_available_mib=med([x['min_mem_available_mib'] for _, x in valid
480:+                                           if x['build'] == build and x['mtp'] == mtp]),
481:+                mean_battery_w=med([(x['power'] or {}).get('mean_battery_w') for _, x in valid
482:+                                    if x['build'] == build and x['mtp'] == mtp]),
483:+                start_skin={n: [x['phases'][n]['start_skin'] for _, x in valid if x['build'] == build and x['mtp'] == mtp]
484:+                            for n in ('cold', 'warm')})
485:+    for c in configs.values():
486:+        print(json.dumps(dict(config=c)), flush=True)
487:+    ratio = {}
488:+    for mtp in (False, True):
489:+        old, new = configs.get(('b1609-dotprod', mtp)), configs.get(('vis', mtp))
490:+        if old and new:
491:+            ratio['mtp' if mtp else 'base'] = {k: round(new[k]/old[k], 3) for k in old
492:+                                               if isinstance(old[k], (int, float)) and not isinstance(old[k], bool)
493:+                                               and old[k] and new[k] is not None and k != 'blocks'}
494:+    # VALID covers execution only. These decide whether OLD and NEW are comparable at all.
495:+    groups = {}
496:+    for _, x in valid:
497:+        groups.setdefault(x['mtp'], {}).setdefault(x['build'], []).append(x)
498:+    comp = {}
499:+    for mtp, g in groups.items():
500:+        xs = [x for v in g.values() for x in v]
501:+        comp['mtp' if mtp else 'base'] = dict(
502:+            both_builds=len(g) == 2, balanced=len({len(v) for v in g.values()}) == 1,
503:+            prompt_n_identical=len({json.dumps(x['prompt_n'], sort_keys=True) for x in xs}) <= 1,
504:+            gen_tokens_identical=len({json.dumps(x['gen_tokens'], sort_keys=True) for x in xs}) <= 1,
505:+            start_skin_spread={n: spread([x['phases'][n]['start_skin'] for x in xs]) for n in ('cold', 'warm')},
506:+            all_checks_pass=all(all(x['checks'].values()) for x in xs))
507:+    print(json.dumps(dict(new_over_old=ratio, comparability=comp)), flush=True)
508:+    return lines, configs, comp
509:+
510:+
511:+def main(argv=None):
512:+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
513:+    ap.add_argument('--blocks', default='O,N,N,O')
514:+    ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
515:+    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
516:+    ap.add_argument('--summarize', type=Path, nargs='+')
517:+    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
518:+    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
519:+    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
520:+    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
521:+    if a.preflight:
522:+        return preflight()
523:+    if a.monitor:
524:+        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
525:+    if a.summarize:
526:+        return summarize(*a.summarize)
527:+    selected = blocks(a.blocks)
528:+    if a.dry_run:
529:+        gen, unc, cached, first = payloads()
530:+        print(DRY_LABEL)
531:+        print(json.dumps(dict(blocks=selected, commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
532:+                              first=first[0], sequence=[(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in sequence(a.smoke, gen, unc, cached)],
533:+                              uncached_chars=len(unc[1]['prompt'])), indent=1))
534:+        return
535:+    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
536:+        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis3/oneshot.sh into Debian')
537:+    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
538:+    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
539:+        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
540:+    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
541:+        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
542:+    check_env()
543:+    v1.free_ports()
544:+    v2.telemetry(bridge)
545:+    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
546:+    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
547:+    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
548:+        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
549:+    gen, unc, cached, first = payloads()
550:+    if any(v1.sha(p) != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
551:+        raise SystemExit('model/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
552:+    idle = None if a.smoke else json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
553:+    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=manifest(selected, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, first), idle_skin=idle,
554:+             note='Short blocks; thermal equivalence between blocks is not established (#93). '
555:+                  + (SMOKE_LABEL if a.smoke else 'ABBA order controls linear drift only.')))
556:+    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
557:+    seq = sequence(a.smoke, gen, unc, cached)
558:+    for i, b in enumerate(selected):
559:+        result = run_block(b, i, out, bridge, idle, seq, first, a.smoke)
560:+        print(f"{i:02d} {b}: {result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
561:+        if result['label'] == 'NOT VALID':
562:+            raise SystemExit('stopped; later blocks not run. Rerun only the missing blocks (README).')
563:+    summarize(out)
564:+    print('Finished. Package RUN_FOLDER (see README); do not rerun for analysis.', flush=True)
565:+
566:+
567:+if __name__ == '__main__':
568:+    try:
569:+        main()
570:+    finally:
571:+        for proc in v1.OWNED:
572:+            v1.own_stop(proc)

592:+v1, v2 = v.v1, v.v2
593:+REPO = v.REPO
594:+T1_LOG = next((REPO/'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/runs/20261019T222505Z').glob('T1_*.server.log'))

597:+
598:+@contextlib.contextmanager
599:+def patched(*triples):
600:+    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
601:+    try:
602:+        for obj, name, value in triples:
603:+            setattr(obj, name, value)
604:+        yield
605:+    finally:
606:+        for obj, name, value in saved:
607:+            setattr(obj, name, value)
608:+
609:+
610:+def deployed_command(setup):
611:+    """The exact shell command server_manager.start_setup builds; os.system and waits are stubbed."""
612:+    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
613:+    import server_manager as sm
614:+    calls = []
615:+    with patched((sm.os, 'system', calls.append), (sm.time, 'sleep', lambda s: None),
616:+                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):
617:+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
618:+            sm.start_setup(setup)
619:+    return shlex.split(calls[-1].replace(' 2>/dev/null &', ''))
620:+

622:+def test_commands_match_deployed_flags():
623:+    prefix = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900']
624:+    for block, setup in (('O', 'setup_q4'), ('OM', 'setup_q4_mtp')):
625:+        cmd = v.command(block)
626:+        assert cmd[:7] == prefix
627:+        want = deployed_command(setup)
628:+        want[want.index('--port') + 1] = '8091'
629:+        assert cmd[7:] == want, (cmd[7:], want)
630:+    o, n, om, nm = (v.command(b) for b in ('O', 'N', 'OM', 'NM'))
631:+    assert o[7] == str(v1.BINS['b1609-dotprod']) and n[7] == str(v1.BINS['vis'])
632:+    assert o[:7] + o[8:] == n[:7] + n[8:], 'only the binary may differ between OLD and NEW'
633:+    assert om == o + v1.DRAFT_ARGS and nm == n + v1.DRAFT_ARGS
634:+    assert '--mmproj' not in ' '.join(nm) and o[o.index('--ctx-size') + 1] == '2048'

710:+def test_gate_absolute_ceiling():
711:+    clock = [0.]
712:+    readings = iter([33.0, 32.4, 31.9])
713:+    tele = lambda bridge: dict(skin=next(readings), status=0, max={}, cpuinfo_max_khz={})
714:+    with patched((v2, 'telemetry', tele)):
715:+        g = v.gate(None, 31.0, now=lambda: clock[0], sleep=lambda s: clock.__setitem__(0, clock[0] + s))
716:+    assert g['skin'] == 31.9 and g['waited_s'] == 10, 'idle+1.5 alone would have passed 32.4'
717:+    assert not v.gate_ok(dict(skin=32.1), 31.0) and v.gate_ok(dict(skin=32.0), 31.0)
718:+    assert not v.gate_ok(dict(skin=30.0), 28.0), 'the VIS-1 idle + 1.5 rule still applies'
719:+    with patched((v2, 'telemetry', lambda b: dict(skin=40.0, status=0))):
720:+        try:
721:+            v.gate(None, 39.0, now=lambda: clock.__setitem__(0, clock[0] + 600) or clock[0], sleep=lambda s: None)
722:+        except RuntimeError as e:
723:+            assert 'gate missed' in str(e)
724:+        else:
725:+            raise AssertionError('gate must give up')

730:+
731:+    def poll(self):
732:+        return None

735:+def fake_run(tmp, fail_verify=False, libs_ok=True):
736:+    out = Path(tmp)
737:+    log = T1_LOG.read_text(errors='replace')
738:+    stops = []
739:+
740:+    def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
741:+        live.update(proc=Proc(), pid=99)
742:+        logpath.write_text(log)
743:+        return 1.5
744:+
745:+    def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
746:+        return dict(kind=kind, input=name, wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10, generated_tokens=5,
747:+                    predicted_per_second=10., prompt_per_second=200., cache_n=0 if kind != 'cached' else 9,
748:+                    reply='ok', draft_n=None, draft_n_accepted=None, freq=None)
749:+
750:+    def verify(block):
751:+        if fail_verify:
752:+            raise RuntimeError('configuration mismatch')
753:+        return dict(vision=False, speculative=[False], n_ctx=[2048], build_info='b')
754:+    tele = dict(skin=30., status=0, capacity=70, voltage_now_uV=4e6, mem_available_kib=1, max={})
755:+    gen, unc, cached, first = v.payloads()
756:+    gates = []
757:+    with patched((v, 'launch', launch), (v, 'timed', timed), (v, 'verify', verify),
758:+                 (v, 'mapped_libs', lambda pid, b: dict(libs=['/elsewhere/libllama.so'], ok=libs_ok)),
759:+                 (v, 'gate', lambda b, i: gates.append(i) or dict(skin=30.)), (v, 'drop_caches', lambda b: None),
760:+                 (v, 'detach', lambda b: True),
761:+                 (v2, 'telemetry', lambda b: tele), (v2, 'fresh_pss', lambda b, p: dict(pss_kib=2048)),
762:+                 (v1, 'check_cores', lambda pid: None), (v1, 'agent_found', lambda log=False: False),
763:+                 (v1, 'own_stop', lambda proc, pid=None: stops.append(pid) or 0), (v1, 'group_alive', lambda g: False),
764:+                 (v1, 'lmk_request', lambda b, e: dict(ok=True)), (v1, 'metrics', lambda r: {})):
765:+        rec = v.run_block('N', 3, out, out, 30., v.sequence(False, gen, unc, cached), first, False)
766:+    rec['gates'] = gates
767:+    return rec, stops, out

770:+def test_run_block_flow():
771:+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
772:+        rec, stops, out = fake_run(tmp)
773:+        files = sorted(p.name for p in out.iterdir())
774:+        saved = json.loads(next(out.glob('03_N_*Z.json')).read_text())
775:+        target = json.loads((out/'target.json').read_text())
776:+    assert rec['label'] == 'VALID', rec['reason']
777:+    assert list(rec['phases']) == ['cold', 'warm'] and stops == [99, 99]
778:+    for ph in rec['phases'].values():
779:+        assert ph['load_s'] == 1.5 and ph['exit_clean'] and ph['first']['kind'] == 'first'
780:+        assert set(ph['markers']) == set(v.MARKERS)
781:+    assert len(rec['requests']) == 3 * v.ROUNDS and 'pss_end' in rec['phases']['warm']
782:+    assert len(rec['gates']) == 3 and 'gate' in rec['phases']['warm'], 'gate, gate after drop, gate before warm'
783:+    assert all(ph['sampler_detached'] for ph in rec['phases'].values())
784:+    assert any(f.endswith('.cold.server.log') for f in files) and any(f.endswith('.warm.server.log') for f in files)
785:+    assert saved['label'] == 'VALID' and target == dict(pid=None)

837:+def record(block, index, build, mtp, gen_tps, load, skin, tokens=5):
838:+    req = lambda kind, name, **kw: dict(dict(kind=kind, input=name, wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10,
839:+                                             generated_tokens=tokens, predicted_per_second=gen_tps,
840:+                                             prompt_per_second=100., cache_n=0, reply='ok', draft_n=None,
841:+                                             draft_n_accepted=None,
842:+                                             freq=dict(policy4=dict(ticks=10, mean_mhz=2000, top_share=1.),
843:+                                                       policy6=dict(ticks=10, mean_mhz=1500, top_share=0.))), **kw)
844:+    phase = lambda: dict(load_s=load, first=req('first', 'F'), markers={}, pss_after_load=dict(pss_kib=3 * 1024 ** 2),
845:+                         exit_clean=True, libs=dict(ok=True), reported=dict(build_info='b'), start=dict(skin=skin))
846:+    return dict(block=block, index=index, build=build, mtp=mtp, label='VALID', reason='', gate=dict(skin=skin),
847:+                phases=dict(cold=phase(), warm=phase()),
848:+                requests=[req('uncached', 'U'), req('cached', 'K0', cache_n=9), req('gen', 'A5')],
849:+                metrics=dict(peak_pss_kib=4 * 1024 ** 2, min_mem_available_kib=2 * 1024 ** 2, power=dict(mean_battery_w=4.)))

852:+def write_folder(root, name, recs, manifest):
853:+    f = Path(root, name)
854:+    f.mkdir()
855:+    (f/'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(manifest=manifest)))
856:+    for r in recs:
857:+        (f/f"{r['index']:02d}_{r['block']}_20261023T000000Z.json").write_text(json.dumps(r))
858:+    (f/'00_O_20261023T000000Z.cold.server.log').write_text('x')
859:+    return f

862:+def test_summarize_pools_folders():
863:+    man = dict(code_sha256={'a': 1}, payload_sha256={}, weight_sha256={}, binary_sha256={}, commands={'O': ['x']},
864:+               rounds=4, first_n=16, skin_start_max=32.0)
865:+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
866:+        f1 = write_folder(tmp, 'r1', [record('O', 0, 'b1609-dotprod', False, 12., 9., 30.),
867:+                                      record('N', 1, 'vis', False, 9., 12., 30.5)], man)
868:+        f2 = write_folder(tmp, 'r2', [record('N', 0, 'vis', False, 9., 12., 31.),
869:+                                      record('O', 1, 'b1609-dotprod', False, 12., 9., 30.2, tokens=6)], man)
870:+        out = io.StringIO()
871:+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
872:+            lines, configs, comp = v.summarize(f1, f2)
873:+        for i, other in enumerate((dict(man, rounds=6), dict(man, commands={'O': ['x', '-ub', '256']}))):
874:+            bad = write_folder(tmp, f'r{3 + i}', [], other)
875:+            try:
876:+                with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
877:+                    v.summarize(f1, bad)
878:+            except SystemExit as e:
879:+                assert 'not pooled' in str(e)
880:+            else:
881:+                raise AssertionError('mismatched manifests must not pool')
882:+    assert len(lines) == 4 and all(x['checks']['libs_ok'] for x in lines)
883:+    old, new = configs[('b1609-dotprod', False)], configs[('vis', False)]
884:+    assert old['blocks'] == new['blocks'] == 2 and old['gen_tok_s'] == 12 and new['load_cold_s'] == 12
885:+    assert old['gen_mean_mhz'] == dict(policy4=2000, policy6=1500)
886:+    last = json.loads(out.getvalue().splitlines()[-1])
887:+    assert last['new_over_old']['base']['gen_tok_s'] == .75 and last['new_over_old']['base']['load_cold_s'] == 1.333
888:+    c = comp['base']
889:+    assert c['both_builds'] and c['balanced'] and c['prompt_n_identical'] and c['all_checks_pass']
890:+    assert c['gen_tokens_identical'] is False and c['start_skin_spread'] == dict(cold=1.0, warm=1.0)
891:+

901:+

1037:+# VIS-3: b1609-dotprod vs b11716 runtime parity (same GGUF, no projector), motors off
1038:+
1039:+This folder only prepares the run; nothing has been measured yet. The owner runs it from native Termux with no agent resident. `vis3_run.py` imports
1040:+`vis2/vis2_run.py` unchanged (and through it `vis1/vis1_run.py`). It reuses their telemetry bridge, battery,
1041:+thermal, abort, agent and affinity stops, cache-drop handshake, owned-process stop, LMK query, PSS and power.
1042:+
1043:+## Why VIS-3 (diagnosis, 2026-10-22, read-only)
1044:+
1045:+The builds are equivalent: same Clang 21.1.8, `-O3`, `-march=armv8.2-a+dotprod+fp16`, OpenMP, REPACK, LLAMAFILE,
1046:+shared libraries, no KleidiAI. Both have `sdot` (1045 vs 1117) and the same 456 FP16 `fmla .8h`. The CMake caches
1047:+differ only in `LLAMA_BUILD_IS_DEV` (version string) and `LLAMA_BUILD_UI`. The effective runtime defaults also match: mmap loading
1048:+(OLD default `mmap`, NEW default `auto` = mmap on CPU, logged `load_mode = mmap`), flash attention `auto` (enabled), batch 2048,
1049:+ubatch 512, `--fit on`, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured. In `common_base_params_to_speculative`, the branch that
1050:+gives the MTP draft the target's threads is the same in both builds, but NEW adds other behaviour to that function. b11716 creates an explicit ggml threadpool. Under OpenMP this
1051:+is plausibly neutral, but that is unverified.
1052:+
1053:+The earlier OLD vs NEW numbers come from different thermal conditions. The VIS gate is idle + 1.5 °C, and session idles were
1054:+27–36.7 °C. VIS-1 T1 (b1609 + MTP) started at skin 39 °C with the X1 capped at 1.58 GHz and ran at 7.07 gen tok/s. VIS-1 T2
1055:+(b11716 + MTP) started at 32.5 °C and ran at 9.22. VIS-2 A (b11716 + MTP) started at 34.2 °C and ran at 7.74. The #137
1056:+chat_mtp run (b1609 + MTP) started at about 24 °C and ran at 19.18. These runs also used different prompts and sampling. None of them
1057:+isolates the build.
1058:+
1059:+## Design
1060:+
1061:+| Block | Binary | MTP drafter |
1062:+|---|---|---|
1063:+| O | `~/llama.cpp-b1609-dotprod` (robot) | off |
1064:+| N | `~/llama.cpp-vis` (b11716) | off |
1065:+| OM / NM (optional) | as O / N | `--model-draft … --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 3` |
1066:+
1067:+The runner and the Debian preflight refuse `LLAMA_*`, `GGML_*`, `OMP_*`, `KMP_*`, `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` and `LD_PRELOAD`
1068:+environment variables, because both servers read hidden settings from them. Each server stops only after the monitor has
1069:+detached from it. A server that maps a library from outside its own build fails the block.
1070:+
1071:+Command = `server_manager.start_setup('setup_q4')` (or `setup_q4_mtp`) exactly, with `taskset -c 4-7`, `timeout 900`
1072:+and port 8091. A test checks this against `server_manager.py`. OLD and NEW differ only in the binary path. No projector
1073:+and no `--reasoning` flag: `/completion` with the raw chat.py prompt never uses the chat template.
1074:+
1075:+Per block: the strengthened gate (skin ≤ idle + 1.5 °C **and** ≤ `SKIN_START_MAX` = 32.0 °C, waiting up to 30 min), then a
1076:+cache drop and the gate again. **Cold phase:** launch, readiness, `/props` and `/slots` checks (no vision, intended MTP,
1077:+n_ctx 2048), the mapped libraries in `/proc/<pid>/maps` must come from the block's own build, PSS, then the first response
1078:+(gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens), then a clean stop. **Warm phase:** the gate again (no cache drop), relaunch (files still cached), the same checks
1079:+and first response, then 4 rounds of U (≈1005-token uncached log, 8 tokens), K (cached prefix, 8 tokens) and G (VIS-1
1080:+prompt, greedy, up to 128 tokens), then a clean stop. Requests are greedy (temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0) and identical
1081:+for every block.
1082:+
1083:+Recorded: cold and warm load time (spawn to `/health` ok) plus log-phase timestamps, first-response wall time, prompt
1084:+and generation tok/s, cache reuse, draft acceptance, and the mean CPU frequency per request from cpufreq `time_in_state`
1085:+(policy4/policy6, no sampling gap). Also recorded: PSS after load and at the end, peak PSS, minimum MemAvailable, battery W,
1086:+skin, Android status, CPU caps, LMK lines, and exit code and stop time.
1087:+
1088:+`VALID` means only that execution conditions held. `--summarize` prints per-config medians, NEW/OLD ratios and the
1089:+comparability checks: both builds present, balanced, identical prompt token counts (tokenization), identical generated
1090:+lengths, cold and warm start-skin spread, and every check passing. The summary pools folders only when their code, payloads, weights, binaries, commands
1091:+and constants match. Draw conclusions only after that assessment.
1092:+
1093:+## Owner commands (native Termux, no agent running, battery ≥ 50 % and unplugged, screen on, Termux in front)
1094:+
1095:+```bash
1096:+bash ~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh --preflight                # readiness only, starts nothing
1097:+bash ~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh --smoke --blocks O,N       # first test, NOT timing (~2 min)
1098:+bash ~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh --blocks O,N,N,O           # essential timed comparison
1099:+bash ~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh --blocks OM,NM,NM,OM       # optional, MTP on both
1100:+```
1101:+
1102:+Expected time for `O,N,N,O`: 5 min idle, then about 4 min active per block (cold + warm load about 25 s, 4 rounds about
1103:+170 s), plus cooling to the gate before each block and each warm phase (typically 5–15 min each). That is about 45–100 min in total. If the phone
1104:+idles above 32 °C the gate cannot pass: cool the room or remove the case. Never raise the ceiling just to make a run pass.
1105:+
1106:+Each launch writes one folder, `vis3/runs/<UTC>/` (or `smoke_<UTC>/`). It holds `run.json` (commands, code, payload,
1107:+weight and binary/library hashes, base commit, git status), one JSON per block, `.cold.server.log` / `.warm.server.log`,
1108:+`samples.jsonl` and `console.log`. A failed block stops the launch and the earlier blocks stay. Rerun only the missing blocks, as a
1109:+new folder (e.g. `--blocks N,O`). The summary pools folders and refuses if their code, payloads, weights, binaries,
1110:+commands or constants differ:
1111:+
1112:+```bash
1113:+python3 -B ~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py --summarize <folder> [<folder> ...]
1114:+```
1115:+
1116:+Short blocks do not establish thermal equilibrium (#93). ABBA cancels linear drift only. These are Termux prototype
1117:+results, not the Android app.

38:def wait_for_server(port=8080, timeout=60):
39:    for i in range(timeout):
40:        time.sleep(1)
41:        try:
42:            r = urllib.request.urlopen(
43:                "http://127.0.0.1:" + str(port) + "/health", timeout=2)
44:            if r.status == 200:
45:                print("[SERVER] Ready after " + str(i+1) + "s")
46:                return True
47:        except:
48:            pass
49:    print("[SERVER] ERROR: failed to load in " + str(timeout) + "s")
50:    return False

1. **P3 — Sampler detachment is best-effort, despite the README’s unconditional guarantee.**

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:197`:
   ```python
   def detach(bridge, wait=5):
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:214`:
   ```python
           phase['sampler_detached'] = detach(bridge)
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:216`:
   ```python
           phase['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
   ```

   A successful acknowledgement resolves the original race: the monitor samples sequentially, so a completed sample begun after clearing the target also establishes that the earlier sample finished. However, shutdown proceeds when acknowledgement times out.

   Concrete scenario: a native sample stalls for more than five seconds before reading the cold server’s `/proc` files. Detachment returns false, shutdown removes those files, and the delayed sample records an error. The warm gate or final sampling-error check can then reject otherwise successful work. This remains fail-closed for measurement validity, so it is nonblocking.

   Make acknowledgement failure stop the block explicitly after owned-server cleanup, and describe the bounded fallback accurately. A delayed-sample lifecycle test would cover this better than the current synthetic future-timestamp test.

The other round 1 findings are addressed: relevant environment overrides are rejected before server launch; pooling compares commands; the warm phase receives its own thermal gate and starting-temperature summary; foreign mapped libraries immediately reject the block; and Debian preflight checks for `requests`.

The whole-candidate review found matching OLD/NEW command arguments, payloads, sampling, affinity, context and output budgets. Cold loading retains the cache-drop handshake; warm loading avoids another drop. Thermal protections remain intact, with the additional absolute ceiling. Owned-process shutdown remains scoped to processes launched by the harness. Summaries exclude invalid/smoke blocks from configuration aggregates and expose comparability separately from execution validity.

The diagnosis is broadly supported by the compiler records, relevant CPU build settings, archived help, draft-thread inheritance and NEW server/projector source. The literal claim that the CMake caches differ *only* in two settings is too broad: NEW also introduces cache entries, including inactive backend options. This does not establish a CPU comparison confound.

The reload proposal was closed on August 11, and the eviction proposal remains a draft focused on GPU memory swapping. [Reload PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25963), [eviction PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28346). I did not independently recount instruction totals, fully reproduce historical thermal figures, verify shallow-clone provenance, or verify issue #30776.

Supplied outputs report VIS-3 17/17 passing, VIS-2 passing, and the disclosed pre-existing VIS-1 wrapper-contract error. My permitted VIS-3 rerun exited 182 without output; these test results were inspected but not independently reproduced. Hardware behavior and timed performance remain for the owner’s later runs.
